:PROJECT:      orgasmic
:TASK:         TASK-4CSMY
:TARGET:       crates/orgasmic-daemon/src/supervisor.rs:4902
:REASON:       HIGH correctness — BLOCKS SHIP: PaneMux now routes tmux captures into pane_open_turn_marker, but this branch accepts any visible line containing Esc to interrupt and lines 4913-4919 accept loose spinner prose anywhere in the capture. An at-rest tmux pane showing prompt, transcript, or tool output with those bytes is classified Working every sweep until the 4-hour max duration, violating the wedge-dies-on-schedule acceptance. TASK-JQ8AV.1 explicitly requires classifier hardening before TASK-4CSMY broadens to tmux; land that hardening first and add real-tmux stale, arbitrary-prose, and forged-marker regressions.
:SEVERITY:     HIGH
:KIND:         correctness
:BLOCKS_SHIP:  true
:REVIEWED_DIFF: 9017514..7d9c60c
:END:

:MACHINE:      Vasyls-MacBook-Pro.local
:PROJECT:      orgasmic
:TASK:         TASK-4CSMY.1
:REASON:       Both reviewer findings closed in supervisor.rs. Marker recognition is now role-aware: STATUS_SPINNER_GLYPHS and TRANSCRIPT_ROLE_GLYPHS replace 'any punctuation glyph' and 'any two-token label', so a completed assistant row at column 0 no longer rescues a wedge. probe_command_stdout shares one absolute deadline with its drain and kills+reaps the group before joining. The author re-measured independently on the live pane of the SIBLING FZB6T.2 worker — a pane neither it nor the reviewer wrote — and confirmed both halves of the reviewer premise. It proved each fix red-first, and the drain red run left a real orphaned sleep 120 reparented to init, i.e. the leak observed rather than argued. Neither verify artifact needed a re-cut, measured by identical hunk offsets with and without the change; manager re-ran both replays independently and both PASS. One criterion partially unmet and declared: the codex TRANSCRIPT bullet is not rejected because it exists nowhere in the repo to measure — the author chose a denylist on that arm so the failure direction is a missed rescue rather than a false kill.

:PROJECT:      orgasmic
:TASK:         TASK-4CSMY
:REASON:       Round 3 found that the tightening reintroduced the original TASK-JQ8AV failure mode. STATUS_SPINNER_GLYPHS omits U+2736 and includes U+2217; the reviewer extracted the frame table from the INSTALLED Claude Code 2.1.220 binary, which returns [B7,2722,2733,2736,273B,273D] normally and [B7,2722,2733,2736,273B,273B] under ghostty — so 2736 is a real frame in both and 2217 is in neither. A provider-bound turn frozen on that frame is now rejected, CPU stays low, and the healthy worker is stall-released. Manager re-ran the same extraction against the same binary and confirms it exactly. The positive controls could not catch it: both real-pane arms hard-code one glyph and the unit loop only proves the table accepts its own entries. The reviewer also cleared two of the author's residuals as acceptable with reasons, and ran the tooling sentinel with the rmux shim stripped, quoting the PATH — so unlike round 2 its coverage claim is checkable.

* TX 2026-08-02 Sun 22:48:13 implementer.done TASK-FZB6T.2
:PROPERTIES:
:TX_ID:        tx-20260802-orgasmic-4164
:TIME:         [2026-08-02 Sun 22:48:13]
:TYPE:         implementer.done
:ACTOR:        agent.implementer
:MACHINE:      Vasyls-MacBook-Pro.local
:PROJECT:      orgasmic
:TASK:         TASK-FZB6T.2
:REASON:       All five HIGH blocking findings and both MEDIUM closed, across two workers. The continuation inherited d982af1 (the WIP the manager preserved when the writer outage killed the first worker) and found it substantially complete but UNGATED — it built clean and its focused suites were already green, and cargo fmt having never run is the marker of exactly where that worker was interrupted. Its own work was the gap between written and provable: fmt, three clippy errors, the finding-6 regression the WIP was killed before writing (surfaced by clippy as an uncalled cfg(test) accessor, then verified red on BOTH halves of the guard independently), and three of four injection artifacts re-cut because this round rewrote the code they inject onto. One of those re-cuts changed the red signature deliberately and the author flagged it for a reviewer rather than quietly re-pinning. Manager re-ran all four replays independently: 4/4 PASS. Two criteria declared unmet, both honestly: no real tmux/rmux production-path test for the redraw ban (driver crate was outside the narrowed write scope), and no full workspace suite (forbidden on this host). :TASK:         TASK-4CSMY.2
:REASON:       Round-3 BLOCK SHIP closed, and the deliverable is the guard rather than the glyph. The author re-ran the binary extraction independently (byte offset 238701938) rather than trusting the reviewer or the manager, and recorded a trap worth keeping: the frames ship as JS escapes, never UTF-8, so grepping the glyph itself returns nothing — a plausible way for a future re-derivation to conclude wrongly. It swept all six other U+2736 sites to confirm the frame arrays are the only spinner. The old positive control was a tautology (it walked the table and asserted the table's own entries), which is precisely how the missing frame survived a review round; the new controls name the frames independently of the table, and a completeness guard streams the installed binary, decodes both sequences and asserts superset. All four of the guard's paths were exercised on this host, including a 1 MiB size floor so the rmux PATH shim cannot trip the needle-absent failure. A real rmux pane now paints the frame and it is proven load-bearing. Gates run with the TASK-NTNK0 PATH correction and the sentinel quoted. No unmet criteria; four residual risks declared.

:TASK:         TASK-FZB6T
:REASON:       Round 2 rejected the residual the previous author declared as acceptable, with a reason: the archive demonstrably predates the append it is claimed to recover, so an append landing between the final fingerprint check and the rename is lost outright. It also found a REGRESSION this fix round introduced — the durable manifest changed its defaulted results array to a defaulted files array with no version and no compatibility decoder, so a current runtime reading an old manifest and an old runtime reading a new one BOTH report a successful rollback having restored nothing, which breaks the exact recoverability contract that justifies deleting evidence. And it proved the new structural scanner accepts invalid JSON: skip_value takes any non-empty primitive token, so a record with a truncated literal is classified rendered_tui and dropped, violating fail-closed. Two MEDIUMs are real: dec_BBPW4 cannot hold both 'catalog is disposable' and 'a tombstone never revives' without durable authority outside the cache, and the re-cut FZB6T.1 injection panics on an earlier path assertion so it never exercises the semantic-validation machinery it now claims to cover — which the manager's own PASSing replay could not have detected.
:MODEL:        gpt-5.6-sol
:EFFORT:       high
:WALL:         18m
:REVIEWED_DIFF: 512d636..0e2c6ea
:MODEL:        gpt-5.6-sol
:EFFORT:       high
:VERDICT:      NEEDS FIXES
:FINDINGS:     3 HIGH blocking (fence is a one-shot handle drop not exclusion held across rename; manifest results->files changed with no version or compat decoder so rollback silently restores nothing in BOTH directions; the structural scanner does not validate JSON so malformed records are classified and deleted), 2 MEDIUM (tombstone durability contradicts the disposable-catalog design; the re-cut FZB6T.1 injection never reaches the semantic-validation assertions it claims to pin)

:PROPERTIES:

:MACHINE:      Vasyls-MacBook-Pro.local
:PROJECT:      orgasmic
:TASK:         TASK-4CSMY
:REASON:       SHIP, no findings, after four rounds on one mechanism. The round-3 blocker is closed: U+2736 accepted, U+2217 no longer claimed as measured, positive controls independent of the production table, and the installed-binary guard fails closed once it identifies a real claude binary. The reviewer re-ran the extraction read-only and confirmed 6 escaped occurrences with ZERO literal UTF-8, corroborating the JS-escape trap, and corrected the cited byte offset — 238701938 points 8 bytes into the unique match that starts at 238701930, though the extractor does not depend on it. It verified the tautology is closed (removing U+2736 now fails WITHOUT relying on the installed binary) and that the real-pane arm is load-bearing. Two residuals were explicitly classified as separate follow-ups rather than round five: a future sub-1-MiB claude launcher would make the audit silent, and the two capture-derived glyphs remain unguarded. Its closing operational note is the one to carry: source shippability is not evidence the installed daemon has these commits — keep the 3600s tmux stall override until the runtime is confirmed rebuilt.

* TX 2026-08-02 Sun 23:14:39 implementer.done TASK-YGB1R
:PROPERTIES:
:TX_ID:        tx-20260802-orgasmic-4214
:TIME:         [2026-08-02 Sun 23:14:39]
:TYPE:         implementer.done
:ACTOR:        agent.implementer
:MACHINE:      Vasyls-MacBook-Pro.local
:PROJECT:      orgasmic
:TASK:         TASK-YGB1R
:REASON:       Three P1 process tasks landed as one coherent text rather than three appends to the same 119 lines. YGB1R gives four countable triggers and a floor rule, deletes the 'if the tier is unclear, ask the user' escape hatch that was the mechanism it existed to remove, and states plainly that the tier is computed so there is no decision to bring the user. 4YX1K defines the confirm class by property with the proceed class named explicitly, and carries the scope-and-expiry nuance verbatim. TW3N3 states the committed-history precondition as one constraint with two halves and adds the branch-collision trap this session hit twice. The calibration table is the check that matters: the rule classifies its own dispatch as ordinary, which is exactly how it was run. Three criteria are unmet and declared — YGB1R item 4 needs TASK-3CM0Q's verb to emit a computed tier, TW3N3 item 3's dispatch guard is Rust and out of scope (filed separately), and TW3N3 item 4 had no grill/review file to extend. The author also corrected the brief: my calibration example described TASK-15MJN as a duplicate cancellation, which is what I DID to it, not what its heading says; it calibrated against the live heading and that mismatch surfaced a real defect in its trigger-2 wording.
:MODEL:        claude-opus-5
:EFFORT:       high

* TX 2026-08-02 Sun 23:50:55 implementer.done TASK-FZB6T.3
:PROPERTIES:
:TX_ID:        tx-20260802-orgasmic-4230
:TIME:         [2026-08-02 Sun 23:50:55]
:TYPE:         implementer.done
:ACTOR:        agent.implementer
:MACHINE:      Vasyls-MacBook-Pro.local
:PROJECT:      orgasmic
:TASK:         TASK-FZB6T.3
:REASON:       All five round-2 findings closed with real machinery rather than narrowed residuals. The fence became a lease held ACROSS the rename — a Session command for a leased path is deferred before anything is opened and runs after the rename and journal write — with a regression that spins on the writer's deferred count rather than sleeping, so it is provably a pre-rename append, and asserts both halves. The manifest is versioned with both shapes decoding, and a plan naming files with no per-file record in ANY readable shape is now refused, which is exactly the shape each runtime saw of the other's and called a successful rollback. The scanner validates per RFC 8259 and is cross-checked against serde_json in BOTH directions, so hardening did not quietly stop reclaiming real payload. dec_BBPW4 took a side: tombstones persist outside the disposable cache, merged never replaced. The split injection was confirmed from the red log rather than inferred. It also fixed six daemon tests that were RED ON MAIN before it started — broken by TASK-FZB6T.2, which I merged and closed — proven by checking 1393e06 into a scratch worktree and reproducing identical panics. My own gate missed that because I ran focused filters to spare the machine; that tradeoff has now been paid once and is recorded.
:MODEL:        claude-opus-5
:EFFORT:       high
:WORKER_COMMIT: 9fff4a2
:MERGE_SHA:    84e4257
:BRANCH:       task-fzb6t.3-impl
:WALL:         37m
:REVIEWED_DIFF: 0bdfaed..84e4257
:MODEL:        claude-opus-5
:EFFORT:       high
:GATES:        fmt clean; clippy -D warnings clean; FULL daemon lib 689/695 with all 6 failures GREEN in isolation (2s, live-mux/process-probe class); core lib 158/0; required_test_tooling_is_present GREEN; author reported 695/0 on a quieter machine
:CLOSED_TX:    tx-20260802-orgasmic-4198

:LIFECYCLE_TO: done

* TX 2026-08-03 Mon 00:47:48 reviewer.done TASK-FZB6T
:PROPERTIES:
:TX_ID:        tx-20260803-orgasmic-4275
:TIME:         [2026-08-03 Mon 00:47:48]
:TYPE:         reviewer.done
:ACTOR:        agent.reviewer
:MACHINE:      Vasyls-MacBook-Pro.local
:PROJECT:      orgasmic
:TASK:         TASK-FZB6T
:REASON:       Round 3. The sharpest finding reopens what round 3 closed: post_run_history_compact holds the SessionLease in the HTTP handler while awaiting spawn_blocking, and a started blocking task is NOT cancelled when its join handle is dropped — so a client disconnect drops the lease while the transaction is still between its final fingerprint check and rename, making the orphaned-inode race live again. The regression cannot catch it because it holds the lease manually. It also found that Rust's is_ascii_whitespace includes 0x0b and 0x0c while JSON permits only four bytes, so a record serde_json rejects is classified reclaimable and deleted — and the 19-case two-way cross-check omits exactly those bytes. This reviewer also served as the TASK-48SFW discriminator and completed untouched, which exonerates the dispatch path.
:MODEL:        gpt-5.6-sol
:EFFORT:       high
:WALL:         20m
:REVIEWED_DIFF: 0bdfaed..84e4257
:MODEL:        gpt-5.6-sol
:EFFORT:       high
:VERDICT:      NEEDS FIXES — BLOCK SHIP
:FINDINGS:     4 P1 blocking (request cancellation drops the lease while spawn_blocking continues; tombstone ledger not fail-closed and neither locked nor fsynced; JSON validator accepts vertical tab and form feed which serde_json rejects; rollback not bound to one validated manifest/archive generation), 1 P2 (machine-specific absolute paths in a repo-root file)
:CLOSED_TX:    tx-20260803-orgasmic-4271
:LIFECYCLE_FROM: in_review
:LIFECYCLE_TO: in_progress
:CLEANUP_STATUS: ok
:END:

:REASON:       All four P1 findings closed with red-then-green regressions, both open questions answered and recorded as drawer properties, and the P2 closed as a consequence of answering question 1. Finding 1 was fixed structurally rather than locally: with_detached_session_lease gives the lease and the blocking transaction one spawned owner, so dropping the caller cancels nothing — the third appearance of the same lifetime-mismatch shape, fixed as a shape. THE AUTHOR CORRECTED THE REVIEWER WITH A MEASUREMENT: finding 3's vertical-tab half does not reproduce, because Rust's is_ascii_whitespace follows WhatWG Infra and excludes 0x0b; the real hole was form feed 0x0c, and both bytes are pinned anyway by a generated differential over every token boundary. It also named a trap that would have voided its own proof — the cancellation test uses Box::pin rather than tokio::pin!, because the latter drops only a Pin<&mut _> and the future survives on the stack.

* TX 2026-08-03 Mon 01:55:08 manager.dispatch_aborted TASK-FZB6T
:PROPERTIES:
:TX_ID:        tx-20260803-orgasmic-4297
:TIME:         [2026-08-03 Mon 01:55:08]
:TYPE:         manager.dispatch_aborted
:ACTOR:        theaspirational@users.noreply.github.com
:MACHINE:      Vasyls-MacBook-Pro.local
:PROJECT:      orgasmic
:TASK:         TASK-FZB6T
:REASON:       Third dispatch-wide outage. Root cause now confirmed IN SOURCE rather than inferred: ensure_running -> start_via_selected_adapter -> daemon_service::start, whose macOS adapter writes and loads ~/Library/LaunchAgents/orgasmic.daemon.plist — a per-user path ORGASMIC_HOME does not scope. Any orgasmic verb that needs a daemon, run against a staged home whose daemon is not up, therefore restarts the REAL daemon and kills every live run. TASK-VEGRV was live in all three outages and died 4-6 minutes in each time, exactly at its render check; the manager's own brief instructed that render check, including the supposedly safe rewrite.
:CLOSED_TX:    tx-20260803-orgasmic-4287
:WORKTREE:     /Users/aspirational/Documents/code/tools/orgasmic/.orgasmic/tmp/dispatch/task-FZB6T-review/worktree
:LIFECYCLE_FROM: in_review
:LIFECYCLE_TO: in_review
:CLEANUP_STATUS: ok
:END:

* TX 2026-08-03 Mon 02:18:57 reviewer.done TASK-FZB6T
:PROPERTIES:
:TX_ID:        tx-20260803-orgasmic-4313
:TIME:         [2026-08-03 Mon 02:18:57]
:TYPE:         reviewer.done
:ACTOR:        agent.reviewer
:MACHINE:      Vasyls-MacBook-Pro.local
:PROJECT:      orgasmic
:TASK:         TASK-FZB6T
:REASON:       Fourth blocking round. The three P1s are each a real resurrect-or-lose-evidence path: boot reattach never consults worktree_authority so a durably tombstoned run can be auto-reattached after a restart even though /api/runs refuses it; refresh_dir applies ledger authority only to freshly rebuilt entries so a cache hit lets stale Verified state beat the durable no-attach fact; and rollback computes its lease from one unlocked manifest read then decodes a different generation under the lock, with no fence assertion, recreating the orphaned-inode window the lease exists to prevent. The P2 is safe-failing — rollback refuses rather than overwriting — but means legacy rollback is unavailable in a classifier-drift case. THE PATTERN IS NOW THE FINDING: every one of these lives in machinery a previous round introduced to satisfy a previous finding.
:MODEL:        gpt-5.6-sol
:EFFORT:       high
:WALL:         12m
:REVIEWED_DIFF: 84e4257..6acad7a
:MODEL:        gpt-5.6-sol
:EFFORT:       high
:VERDICT:      NEEDS FIXES — BLOCK SHIP
:FINDINGS:     3 P1 blocking (boot reattach bypasses tombstoned/unprovable authority; ledger authority ignored on catalog cache hits; rollback leases paths from a different manifest generation than it executes), 1 P2 safe-failing (format-1 rollback re-derives with the current compactor)
:PATTERN:      round 4 of 4; findings 1-2 are in the tombstone ledger ADDED by round 3, finding 3 is in the lease/rollback interaction CHANGED by round 3
:CLOSED_TX:    tx-20260803-orgasmic-4303

And it caught a FALSE GREEN on a shipped artifact: the TASK-FZB6T.1 injection stopped reproducing one of its three pinned reds because finding 2's fix re-tombstones on the same refresh pass, so rather than accept a passing verify it re-cut the patch to restore the tree state the .1 defect was observed in. Residual declared: two operator-initiated recovery reattach call sites still do not consult blocks_attach(), judged a product decision about whether an operator may force a recovery.

:REASON:       SHIP, no findings, after five fix rounds and five review rounds. It verified the load-bearing claim rather than the findings alone: the no-new-mechanism constraint HOLDS — the delta adds no file, lock, state enum, struct or state container, and reconcile_authority_with_ledger extracts an existing rule while FencedSessions and the detached lease owner are reused unchanged. That is the evidence the defer-or-continue decision needed, and it says the subsystem converged rather than kept growing. It also confirmed the re-cut TASK-FZB6T.1 artifact reaches its pinned 0-passed-3-failed signature through the RIGHT assertions, naming the three line numbers where those messages are the FIRST failing assertion rather than a shared earlier panic — which is the check the false-green episode made necessary. Two residual test gaps declared and judged not to change the verdict, because the production wiring is single-path and was inspected end to end.

* TX 2026-08-03 Mon 03:16:07 implementer.done TASK-3CM0Q
:PROPERTIES:
:TX_ID:        tx-20260803-orgasmic-4341
:TIME:         [2026-08-03 Mon 03:16:07]
:TYPE:         implementer.done
:ACTOR:        agent.implementer
:MACHINE:      Vasyls-MacBook-Pro.local
:PROJECT:      orgasmic
:TASK:         TASK-3CM0Q
:REASON:       The verb exists and the default is inverted. Its best decision is the asymmetry on re-declaration: growth re-declares upward with no flag and no permission, because the tier is computed from countable triggers and a recomputation is not a decision anyone can approve — but LOWERING is refused without --lower and a reason stating the first declaration was wrong, since 'it looked smaller once I was inside it' is the exact rationalization the floor rule exists to stop and must not be spellable as a fresh computation. On the inherited WIP it found the daemon half complete but entirely unwired with no test, and the CLI verb non-existent; it verified the handler against the acceptance rather than trusting it. It also found the SAFE way to drive a real daemon from a test — ORGASMIC_DAEMON_URL on the child makes ensure_running return before any adapter start — which is the pattern TASK-48SFW's three outages should have used, and the manager confirmed the plist mtime was unchanged after running it.
:MODEL:        claude-opus-5
:EFFORT:       high
:MERGE_SHA:    40d5229
:BRANCH:       task-3cm0q-impl-cont
:WALL:         22m
:REVIEWED_DIFF: aa400dc..HEAD
:MODEL:        claude-opus-5
:EFFORT:       high

:REASON:       Closed ABORTED rather than done because no code was written and there is nothing to merge — but the run was a success, not a failure, and it corrects ME. TASK-TJKFC was already COMPLETE: its heading opens with PARTIAL IMPLEMENTATION and ends ~250 lines later with a COMPLETE block superseding every earlier NOT LANDED list, and I scoped the brief from the first section without reading to the end. That misrouted a dispatch on 2026-07-26 and again today. Rather than write duplicate code, the worker reproduced every claim by RUNNING its tests: atomic abort (f80b577), the race test driven by a driver pushing fatal synchronously inside acquire, and the end-to-end test proving 400 with no session, no lease, no run.created, and a second dispatch refused 400 not 409 so the lease is genuinely free. All green, no billed turn. It also declined fmt/clippy/full-suite with reasoning: it changed no source, and the machine was not calm because I had dispatched 8ED6V concurrently — the exact condition my own brief names as making those reds spurious.

* TX 2026-08-03 Mon 04:30:12 implementer.done TASK-8ED6V
:PROPERTIES:
:TX_ID:        tx-20260803-orgasmic-4367
:TIME:         [2026-08-03 Mon 04:30:12]
:TYPE:         implementer.done
:ACTOR:        agent.implementer
:MACHINE:      Vasyls-MacBook-Pro.local
:PROJECT:      orgasmic
:TASK:         TASK-8ED6V
:REASON:       One table in core owns the retired-content families and every surface reads it, so a future cutover is one edit rather than four to remember. The maintenance discipline is ENFORCED rather than documented: a test fails when a table entry is not named in the shipped router with its deciding node, so a cutover author cannot land the code without the agent-facing text in the same release. The author declined the more elegant :RETIRES_PATHS: decision-property trigger with a real reason — a dispatch worktree's .orgasmic/ is a frozen snapshot, so that test would pass vacuously on the branch and fail after merge. Removal is a separate flag rather than part of --fix because --fix repairs runtime-owned things while this deletes operator-written content, and for the same reason it is not on the upgrade path. Manager verified the doctor output behaviourally against staged residue rather than trusting the tests.
:MODEL:        claude-opus-5
:EFFORT:       high
:MERGE_SHA:    96c1f9d
:BRANCH:       task-8ed6v-impl
:WALL:         10m

:MODEL:        claude-opus-5
:EFFORT:       high
:GATES:        fmt clean; clippy -D warnings clean; retired_content 6/0; cli_parity 3/0; core fixtures 26/0; cli doctor 2/0; behaviour verified on the BUILT binary with staged residue, not on the installed one
:CLOSED_TX:    tx-20260803-orgasmic-4351
